--- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 5135
entity_slug: shrimpys-grill-and-golf
google_review_name: places/ChIJ-czdfbcEmogRrXSPDhnJt7M/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT25nMGVtTXlObDl1T0hBdFFXRXhRa0pNVUhoWWVWRRAB
rating: 4
review_text: Shrimpy’s was an okay experience. The mini golf was on par with most smaller golf places around the area. But don’t be fooled by “it’s the cheapest”. We had 6 people and brought the “buy 1 get 1 half-off” coupon, thinking we would pay full price for 3 and half price for 3 - thus, the most affordable golf in the area. WRONG. We had to pay full price for 5 adults and the guy took the 1/2 price off the one kid player. Lame.

We also ate there. I felt bad b/c there seemed to be only one guy working alone. The “loaded” parm fries that we paid extra for were disappointing, the blackened fish sandwich was *meh*. The flatbread and mozzarella sticks were surprisingly good. My husband liked his burger “fine” and chicken tenders were also fine - standard grill food.

The only reason i gave it a 4th star was b/c it is probably the most affordable alcohol in the area. An ON TAP fairhope blueberry lemon pale ale for $3.50?? Unheard of on the beach. And most mixed drinks were $3.50, too.

If you have just a few people and wanna drink and play mini golf, this is a win. If you have a bigger fam like us, i don’t recommend spending your money here.

--- entity_google_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
review_id: 5137
entity_slug: shrimpys-grill-and-golf
google_review_name: places/ChIJ-czdfbcEmogRrXSPDhnJt7M/reviews/ChdDSUhNMG9nS0VJQ0FnSUQydEszSW53RRAB
rating: 4
review_text: Very well maintained mini-18 hole golf course. It's fun, attractive and just enough time to go through the course.

The ice cream shop and the dining area is well kept, there are clean restrooms inside.
They charge $10 for adult and $7 for kids, you could pickup the clubs and the golf ball inside along with scorecard with pencil.

The 18-hole mini golf course is nice, well maintained and is easy to navigate through.

The parking area is a bit small, could accommodate few cars, it's a challenge during busy hours.

The ice cream and the overall customer service would need a push.

It's a fun place to spend an hour or two with your loved ones.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 57e8613d-759f-4fe8-9dbe-8bc707f4f5eb
entity_slug: shrimpys-grill-and-golf
reviewer_name: Rebecca Murphy
rating: 4
body: Shrimpy’s was an okay experience. The mini golf was on par with most smaller golf places around the area. But don’t be fooled by “it’s the cheapest”. We had 6 people and brought the “buy 1 get 1 half-off” coupon, thinking we would pay full price for 3 and half price for 3 - thus, the most affordable golf in the area. WRONG. We had to pay full price for 5 adults and the guy took the 1/2 price off the one kid player. Lame.

We also ate there. I felt bad b/c there seemed to be only one guy working alone. The “loaded” parm fries that we paid extra for were disappointing, the blackened fish sandwich was *meh*. The flatbread and mozzarella sticks were surprisingly good. My husband liked his burger “fine” and chicken tenders were also fine - standard grill food.

The only reason i gave it a 4th star was b/c it is probably the most affordable alcohol in the area. An ON TAP fairhope blueberry lemon pale ale for $3.50?? Unheard of on the beach. And most mixed drinks were $3.50, too.

If you have just a few people and wanna drink and play mini golf, this is a win. If you have a bigger fam like us, i don’t recommend spending your money here.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2025-07-29T22:39:19.345846+00:00

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 1d86078f-ea36-45e2-85c2-1e3343212335
entity_slug: shrimpys-grill-and-golf
reviewer_name: Madan Nagaraju
rating: 4
body: Very well maintained mini-18 hole golf course. It's fun, attractive and just enough time to go through the course.

The ice cream shop and the dining area is well kept, there are clean restrooms inside.
They charge $10 for adult and $7 for kids, you could pickup the clubs and the golf ball inside along with scorecard with pencil.

The 18-hole mini golf course is nice, well maintained and is easy to navigate through.

The parking area is a bit small, could accommodate few cars, it's a challenge during busy hours.

The ice cream and the overall customer service would need a push.

It's a fun place to spend an hour or two with your loved ones.
